country_event = { #The army pressures Hindenburg
#In his reports to Hindenburg, Blomberg wrote that his arch-rival Schleicher's attempts to create the Wehrstaat had clearly failed, and that Germany needed a new approach to forming the Wehrstaat.
#By late January 1933, it was clear that the Schleicher government could only stay in power by proclaiming martial law and by authorizing the Reichswehr to crush popular opposition. 
#In doing so, the military would have to kill hundreds, if not thousands of German civilians; any régime established in this way could never expect to build the national consensus necessary to create the Wehrstaat.
#The military had decided that Hitler alone was capable of peacefully creating the national consensus that would allow the creation of the Wehrstaat, and thus the military successfully brought pressure on Hindenburg to appoint Hitler as Chancellor.
#Blomberg served as one of the main channels by which the Reichswehr informed Hindenburg of their wish to see Hitler become Chancellor.
	id = ger_army.0
	title = ger_army.0.t
	desc = ger_army.0.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_hindenburg_with_chiefs
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = ger_army.0.a
		
	}
}

unit_leader_event = { #Hammerstein-Equord warns Hindenburg
#As close friend of Kurt von Schleicher, Hammerstein repeatedly warned President Paul von Hindenburg about the dangers of appointing Adolf Hitler, leader of the National Socialist German Workers' Party, as chancellor. 
#In response, Hindenburg assured Hammerstein-Equord that "he would not even consider making that Austrian corporal the minister of defense or the chancellor".
#Barely four days later, on 30 January 1933, pursuant to a request by Hindenburg, Hitler formed a cabinet as the German Chancellor in a coalition of the NSDAP and the German National People's Party.
	id = ger_army.1
	title = ger_army.1.t
	desc = ger_army.1.d
	picture = x
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = ger_army.1.a
		
	}
}

country_event = { # Blomberg recalled to Berlin
#In late January 1933, President Hindenburg—without informing the chancellor, Schleicher, or the army commander, General Kurt von Hammerstein—recalled Blomberg from the World Disarmament Conference to return to Berlin.
#Upon learning of this, Schleicher guessed correctly that the order to recall Blomberg to Berlin meant his own government was doomed.
#When Blomberg arrived at the railroad station in Berlin on 28 January 1933, he was met by two officers, Adolf-Friedrich Kuntzen and Oskar von Hindenburg, adjutant and son of President Hindenburg. Kuntzen had orders from Hammerstein for Blomberg to report at once to the Defense Ministry, while Oskar von Hindenburg had orders for Blomberg to report directly to the Palace of the Reich President.
#Over and despite Kuntzen's protests, Blomberg chose to go with Hindenburg to meet the president, who swore him in as defense minister.
#This was done in a manner contrary to the Weimar constitution, under which the president could only swear in a minister after receiving the advice of the chancellor.
#Hindenburg had not consulted Schleicher about his wish to see Blomberg replace him as defense minister because in late January 1933, there were wild (and untrue) rumors circulating in Berlin that Schleicher was planning to stage a putsch.
#To counter alleged plans of a putsch by Schleicher, Hindenburg wanted to remove Schleicher as defense minister as soon as possible.
#Two days later, on 30 January 1933, Hindenburg swore in Adolf Hitler as Chancellor, after telling him that Blomberg was to be his defense minister regardless of his wishes.
	id = ger_army.2
	title = ger_army.2.t
	desc = ger_army.2.d
	picture = GFX_event_werner_von_blomberg
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = GER_werner_von_blomberg
				traits = { old_guard career_officer reichwehr_general }
				legacy_id = 36
				skill = 3
				attack_skill = 3
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 3
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
			remove_ideas = coa_schleicher
			add_ideas = coa_blomberg
			country_event = {
				id = ger_army.3
				days = 272
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ger_army.4
				days = 246
				random_days = 28
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ger_army.5
				days = 369
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ger_army.13
				days = 1178
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_army.2.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = coa_schleicher
				add_idea = coa_blomberg
			}
		}
	}
}

	fire_only_once = yes

		}

			set_country_flag = GER_adam_sacked

			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = wilhelm_adam
				add_idea = cos_ludwig_beck
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_army.3.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = wilhelm_adam
				add_idea = cos_ludwig_beck
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Hammerstein-Equord resigns. October 1933
#The British historian Sir John Wheeler-Bennett wrote about the "ruthless" way that Blomberg set about isolating and undermining the power of the army commander-in-chief, a close associate of Schleicher, General Kurt von Hammerstein-Equord, to the point that in February 1934 Hammerstein finally resigned in despair, as his powers had become more nominal than real.
#With Hammerstein's resignation, the entire Schleicher faction that had dominated the army since 1926 had been removed from their positions within the High Command

#Because of his opposition to Hitler, Hammerstein-Equord tendered his resignation in October 1933. It was accepted in December and became effective on 31 January 1934. He was succeeded by General Werner von Fritsch.
	id = ger_army.4
	title = ger_army.4.t
	desc = ger_army.4.d
	picture = x
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_government = fascism
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		NOT = { has_global_flag = GCW_ongoing_flag }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_army.4.a
		
	}
}

country_event = { #
#Hammerstein-Equord resignation becomes effective 31 Jannuary 1933. Event Fires 1 day later, the same day von Fritsch was appointed.
#The resignation of Hammerstein caused a crisis in military-civil relations when Hitler attempted to appoint as his successor Reichenau, a man who was not acceptable to the majority of the Reichswehr.
#Blomberg supported the attempt to appoint Reichenau, but reflecting the power of the "state within the state", certain Army officers appealed to Hindenburg, which led to Werner von Fritsch being appointed instead.
	id = ger_army.5
	title = ger_army.5.t
	desc = ger_army.5.d
	picture = x
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		has_government = fascism
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		NOT = { has_global_flag = GCW_ongoing_flag }
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = GER_kurt_hammerstein_equord
				}
				retire_character = GER_kurt_hammerstein_equord
			}
			set_country_flag = GER_fritsch_chief_of_army
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = chief_hammerstein_equord
				add_idea = cos_werner_von_fritsch
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_army.5.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = chief_hammerstein_equord
				add_idea = cos_werner_von_fritsch
			}
		}
		add_ideas = manstein_chief
	}
}

country_event = { #Blomberg is promoted to Generalfeldmarschall. Werner von Fritsch is promoted to Generaloberst to fill Blomberg's vacated rank of colonel general
	# 20 April 1936
	id = ger_army.13
	title = ger_army.13.t
	desc = ger_army.13.d
	picture = GFX_event_werner_von_blomberg
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		has_character = GER_werner_von_blomberg
		GER_werner_von_blomberg = { is_unit_leader = yes }
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_army.13.a
		GER_werner_von_blomberg = {
			promote_leader = yes
			remove_unit_leader_trait = wehrmacht_general
		}
		add_corps_commander_role = {
			character = GER_werner_von_fritsch
			traits = { old_guard wehrmacht_general }
			legacy_id = 37
			skill = 3
			attack_skill = 2
			defense_skill = 1
			planning_skill = 3
			logistics_skill = 2
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Blomberg–Fritsch affair
	#https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blomberg%E2%80%93Fritsch_affair
	#12 January 1938
	id = ger_army.10
	title = ger_army.10.t
	desc = ger_army.10.d
	picture = x
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = GER_fritsch_chief_of_army
			remove_ideas = coa_blomberg
			remove_ideas = cos_werner_von_fritsch
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_army.10.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			remove_ideas = coa_blomberg
			remove_ideas = cos_werner_von_fritsch
			retire_character = GER_ewald_von_kleist
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character = GER_werner_von_blomberg
			}
			retire_character = GER_werner_von_blomberg
		}
		add_ideas = coa_walther_von_brauchitsch
		hidden_effect = {
			GER_ewald_von_kleist = {
				set_character_flag = GER_kleist_retired
				remove_unit_leader_role = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = ger_internal.1
	title = ger_internal.1.t
	desc = ger_internal.1.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_Hindenburg
	timeout_days = 2

	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = neutrality
				long_name = GER_no_party_long
				name = GER_no_party
			}

			random_list = { #TODO: Hindenburg events that lead or not to the disaster
				80 = {
					country_event = {
						id = ger_flavor.1
						days = 1559
					}
				}
				20 = {
					country_event = {
						id = ger_flavor.2
						days = 1559
					}
				}
			}

			#Nazi Events
			country_event = { #Reichstag Fire
				id = ger_flavor.22
				days = 29
			}
			country_event = { #5 march 1933 election
				id = ger_flavor.7
				days = 34
			}
			country_event = { #Triumph des Willens
				id = ger_flavor.3
				days = 789
			}
			country_event = { #Confessing Church
				id = ger_flavor.12
				days = 461
			}
			country_event = { #hindenburg dies
				id = ger_internal.8
				days = 549
			}

			#DNVP events
			country_event = { #hindenburg dies DNVP
				id = ger_internal.33
				days = 549
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Far-Right Candidate
		ai_chance = {
			base = 75
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				add = 100
			}
			modifier = {
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = GER_select_path
					option = GER_WGR_PREFERENCE
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		name = ger_internal.1.a
		country_event = { id = ger_internal.2 }
	}
	option = { #Democratic Candidate
		name = ger_internal.1.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 25
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = GER_select_path
						option = GER_FAR_RIGHT_PREFERENCE
					}
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = GER_select_path
						option = GER_NAZI_PREFERENCE
					}
					has_game_rule = {
						rule = GER_select_path
						option = GER_DNVP_PREFERENCE
					}
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = ger_internal.3 }
	}
	#option = {

	id = ger_internal.2

			}
		}
	}

			factor = 60
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = {
					rule = GER_select_path
					option = GER_DNVP_PREFERENCE
				}
			}
		}
		
		set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism elections_allowed = no }

		COALITION_add_mon = yes

		remove_ideas_with_trait = ecomin

		# 	keep_completed = yes
		# }

		add_stability = 0.05

			GER_kurt_hammerstein_equord = {
				add_unit_leader_trait = hidden_sympathies
			}
			set_portraits = {

	}

	option = {

country_event = {
	id = ger_internal.3
	title = ger_internal.3.t
	desc = ger_internal.3.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_German_Cabinet
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_internal.3.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = GER_swtich_to_wgr
		hidden_effect = {
			64 = {
				transfer_state_to = WGR
				add_core_of = WGR
			}
			GER = {
				set_nationality = {
					target_country = WGR
					character = GER_paul_von_hindenburg
				}
				every_unit_leader = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							is_character = GER_wilhelm_list
							is_character = GER_oswald_lutz
							is_character = GER_kurt_hammerstein_equord
							has_id = 31
						}
					}
					add_unit_leader_trait = germany_rightist
				}
				remove_ideas = coa_schleicher
				remove_ideas = chief_hammerstein_equord
				remove_ideas = wilhelm_adam
				remove_ideas_with_trait = hog
				remove_ideas_with_trait = frnmin
				remove_ideas_with_trait = ecomin
				remove_ideas_with_trait = secmin
				remove_ideas_with_trait = intmin
				set_nationality = {
					target_country = WGR
					character = GER_adolf_hitler
				}

################################################################
##### Oster Conspiracy
##### An OTL - 1938 conspiracy to kill Hitler should he go to war with CZE.
##### Simulated As Such:
# The Sudetenland Focus will trigger ger_internal.16, in which Oster and his conspirators will start to meet.
## This sets the GER_Oster_Goes_Forwards flag
# If there is a war with CZE over the sudetenland, then either the conspiracy will be detected (40%) or it will go forward (60%)
# If it goes forwards, there are three options:
### Full success: Leader dies, full white peace is made, Ludwig Beck takes control. Foreign policy is now practically disabled.
### Partial Success: Leader dies, war continues, Germany suffers with -1.0 encryption and decryption for a year.
### Failure: Leader survives, war continues, Germany suffers with -1.0 encryption and decryption for a half year
## At the end, the government either establishes a Monarchy or continues a military dictatorship.

#In the autumn of 1939, Beck was in contact with German Army officers, politicians, and civil servants, including General Halder, Dr. Hjalmar Schacht, Carl Goerdeler, Admiral Wilhelm Canaris and Colonel Hans Oster about the possibility of staging a putsch to overthrow the Nazi regime.
#By then, Beck had come to accept that it was not possible to overthrow the Nazi regime if that meant keeping Hitler in power. 
#After a successful putsch, Germany was to be governed by a triumvirate of Beck, Goerdeler and Schacht, who would negotiate a peace with Britain and France that would allow Germany to keep most of its conquests, including Austria, all of western Poland and the Reich Protectorate of Bohemia-Moravia.
#######################

# Make sure WGR and DDR agree
# Make sure flags agree

	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_internal.16.a
		set_country_flag = GER_Oster_Goes_Forwards
	}
}
country_event = { # Oster Conspiracy Goes Forwards!
	id = ger_internal.17
	title = ger_internal.17.t
	desc = ger_internal.17.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Oster_Conspiracy #Oster_Conspiracy
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = GER
		has_war_with = CZE
		has_government = fascism
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
	}
	option = { # full success
		name = ger_internal.17.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 25
		}
		add_ideas = Legacy_of_Hitler
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		retire_character = GER_adolf_hitler
		clr_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_war_support = -0.15
		add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.08 }
		set_country_flag = GER_Oster_full_success
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = "GER_ludwig_beck"
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = military
				expire = "1965.1.1.1"
				traits = {}
			}
		}
		set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no }
		add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.15 }
		load_focus_tree ={
			tree = german_focus_hitler_strasser_military_coup
			keep_completed = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = hitler }
				remove_ideas = hitler
			}
		}
		remove_ideas_with_trait = frnmin
		remove_ideas_with_trait = ecomin
		remove_ideas_with_trait = secmin
		remove_ideas_with_trait = intmin
		add_ideas = {
			hog_beck #goerdeler2?
			schulenberg
			hjalmar_schacht
			tresckow
			canaris
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_defensive_war_with = GER
			}
			white_peace = GER
		}
	}
	
	option = { # partial success - leader dies, regime survives
		name = ger_internal.17.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 35
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.2
				factor = 0.5
			}
		}
		retire_character = GER_adolf_hitler
		clr_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = hitler }
			remove_ideas = hitler
		}
		add_ideas = hess
		add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.08 }
		set_country_flag = GER_Oster_partial_success
		set_country_flag = GER_oster_incapable
		add_political_power = -50
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = canaris }
			remove_ideas = canaris
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = hog_beck }
			remove_ideas = hog_beck
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = cos_ludwig_beck }
			remove_ideas = cos_ludwig_beck
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = GER_ewald_von_kleist }
			retire_character = GER_ewald_von_kleist
		}
	}

	option = { # failure
		name = ger_internal.17.c
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 40
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.2
				factor = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.5
				factor = 0.2
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = GER_Oster_failure
		set_country_flag = GER_oster_incapable
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = -0.08 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = canaris }
			remove_ideas = canaris
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = hog_beck }
			remove_ideas = hog_beck
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = GER_ewald_von_kleist }
			retire_character = GER_ewald_von_kleist
		}
	}
}

###############################################
############### July 20 Plot ###################
#### A 1944 conspiracy to kill Hitler by Henning Von Treskow, Claus von Stauffenberg, and others.
#### Simulated as Such:
#### It starts at about July 1944 OR if Germany is getting occupied
#### Should it go forward, then there would be an event determining Hitler's Status.
#### If he is alive (historical), then the conspirators would be murdered.
#### If he dies, the next event details Goring's attempt to take over the leadership, and gives an order to Major Remer to arrest the conspirators.
#### Remer could comply, leading to partial success
#### Remer could shoot Goring, leading to full success
#### Unlike the Oster Conspiracy, this only occurs if Hitler is in control
# 19 - Hitler Alive/Dead
# 20 - Goring demands on Remer
# 21 - Coup Succeeds
# 22 - Coup Fails

#Ludwig Beck
#In 1943, Beck planned two abortive attempts to kill Hitler by means of a bomb. In May 1944, a memorandum by Field Marshal Erwin Rommel made it clear that his participation in the proposed putsch was based on the condition of Beck serving as the head of state in the new government.
#In 1944, he was one of the driving forces of the 20 July plot, along with Carl Goerdeler and Colonel Claus von Stauffenberg. It was proposed that Beck would become Reichsverweser (regent) and head of the provisional government that would assume power in Germany after Hitler had been eliminated


country_event = { # Hitler's status
	id = ger_internal.19
	title = ger_internal.19.t
	desc = ger_internal.19.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Operation_Valkyrie #Operation Valkyrie
	
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { # Alive
		name = ger_internal.19.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 97
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.2
				factor = 0.8
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.5
				factor = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.75
				factor = 0.5
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = GER_july_20_hitler_lives
		add_stability = -0.05
		country_event = { id = ger_internal.22 days = 1 }
	}
	option = { # Dead
		name = ger_internal.19.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 3
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = GER_july_20_hitler_dies
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_war_support = -0.10
		kill_country_leader = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = hitler }
			remove_ideas = hitler
			add_ideas = goring
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = coa_adolf_hitler }
			remove_ideas = coa_adolf_hitler
		}
		#TODO: hitler death: should be donitz
		promote_character = GER_hermann_goring
		country_event = { id = ger_internal.20 }
	}
}

country_event = { # Hitler is dead, demands Remer to arrest the other conspirators
	id = ger_internal.20
	title = ger_internal.20.t
	desc = ger_internal.20.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Otto_Ernst_Remer #Otto_Ernst_Remer

country_event = { #Berlin is Taken, hitler kills himself
	id = ger_internal.23
	title = ger_internal.23.t
	desc = ger_internal.23.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Destroyed_Fuhrerbunker #Destroyed_Fuhrerbunker
	
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes 

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			retire_character = GER_adolf_hitler
			retire_character = GER_joseph_goebbels #He comits die
			set_global_flag = GER_Hitler_Kaput
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = hitler
				}
				remove_ideas = hitler
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = coa_adolf_hitler
				}
				remove_ideas = coa_adolf_hitler
				add_ideas = coa_ferdinand_schorner
				#Ferdinand Schörner becomes chief of the Army
			}

			#President
			if = { #Hess becomes President
				limit = {
					has_character = GER_rudolf_hess
				}
				promote_character = GER_rudolf_hess
			}
			else_if = { #Dönitz becomes President
				limit = {
					has_character = GER_karl_donitz
				}
				add_country_leader_role = {
					character = "GER_karl_donitz"
					promote_leader = yes
					country_leader = {
						ideology = nazism
						expire = "1965.1.1.1"
						traits = {}
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = { #Göring becomes President
				limit = { has_character = GER_hermann_goring }
				promote_character = GER_hermann_goring
			}

			#Chancellor
			#Goebbels should be Chancellor but he immediately kills himself
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = GER_martin_bormann }
				add_ideas = bormann #Bormann becomes Chancellor
			}
			else = {
				add_ideas = krosigk
			}

			news_event = { #Hitler Dead News Event
				id = ger_news.100
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}

	#https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Last_will_and_testament_of_Adolf_Hitler
	
	option = {
		name = ger_internal.23.a
		
		# add_ideas = {
		# 	GER_Hitler_is_dead
		# }	
		
		add_war_support = -0.3
		add_stability = -0.25
	}
}

				}

country_event = { #Elections
	id = ger_internal.63
	title = ger_internal.63.t
	desc = ger_internal.63.d
	picture = GFX_Events_Germany_Plebiscite_Results
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_government = monarchism
		tag = GER
		has_country_flag = GER_Goerdeler_Elections
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_country_flag = GER_DNVP_Parliament
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_variable = WGR_supseat
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					WGR_supseat = 0
				}
			}
			set_variable = {
				GER_total_seats = 498
			}
			GER_Goerdeler_Set_Win_Size_Party = yes
			GER_Goerdeler_Set_Victorious_Party = yes
			country_event = {
				id = ger_internal.63
				days = 1825
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_variable = GER_intrest_group_count
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					GER_intrest_group_count = 0
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ger_internal.65
					days = 23
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #
		name = ger_internal.63.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					Winning_Group > 2.499 #rightist victory
				}
			}
			GER_Rightist_Victory = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { # Centre Rightist
					Winning_Group < 2.5
				}
				check_variable = {
					Winning_Group > 1.749
				}
			}
			GER_Centre_Rightist_Victory = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { #centrists
					Winning_Group < 1.75
				}
				check_variable = {
					Winning_Group > 1.249
				}
			}
			GER_Centre_Victory = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					Winning_Group < 1.25
				}
				check_variable = {
					Winning_Group > 0.499
				}
			}
			GER_Centre_Leftist_Victory = yes
		}
		else = {
			GER_Leftist_Victory = yes
		}
		GER_Set_Parliament_Conservative_Bias = yes
		COALITION_add_RIGHTIST = yes
	}
}

country_event = { #inflation
	id = ger_internal.67
	title = ger_internal.67.t
	desc = ger_internal.67.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Reichsbank_Commission
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_internal.67.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_idea = goerdeler
					has_idea = goerdeler2
				}
				factor = 0.2
			}
		}
		add_ideas = GER_DNVP_Inflation
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				tempRand = random
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				tempRand = 100
			}
			round_variable = tempRand
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						tempRand < 25.01
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ger_internal.69
					days = 2800
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						tempRand > 25
					}
					check_variable = {
						temp_rand < 50.01
					}
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ger_internal.70
					days = 2800
				}
			}
			else = {
				country_event = {
					id = ger_internal.68
					days = 330
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_internal.67.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
		}
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_idea = goerdeler
				has_idea = goerdeler2
			}
		}
		add_ideas = GER_DNVP_Inflation
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = free_trade
			}
			add_political_power = 25
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = export_focus
			}
			add_political_power = -100
			add_ideas = free_trade
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = limited_exports
			}
			add_political_power = -100
			add_ideas = export_focus
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = closed_economy
			}
			add_political_power = -100
			add_ideas = limited_exports
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = ger_internal.69
				days = 150
			}
		}
	}
}

				planning_skill = 3
				logistics_skill = 3
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				GER_maximilian_von_weichs = {
					is_unit_leader = yes
					has_trait = corps_commander_trait
				}
			}
			GER_maximilian_von_weichs = { remove_unit_leader_trait = corps_commander_trait }
		}
	}
}

###############################################
############### SS Coup Plot ###################
#### This should start if Germany is getting occupied from the west before the east
#### If Hitler is alive , then the conspirators would be murdered.
#### If Hitler dies, the SS takes over
#### This should be expanded on in the future
# 92 - Hitler Alive/Dead
country_event = {
	id = ger_internal.92
	title = ger_internal.92.t
	desc = ger_internal.92.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Destroyed_Fuhrerbunker
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = { #Hitler is dead
		name = ger_internal.92.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
		retire_character = GER_adolf_hitler
		clr_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		set_global_flag = GER_Hitler_Kaput
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = hitler
			}
			remove_ideas = hitler
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = coa_adolf_hitler
			}
			remove_ideas = coa_adolf_hitler
			add_ideas = coa_ferdinand_schorner
			#Ferdinand Schörner becomes chief of the Army
		}
		add_stability = -0.20
		load_focus_tree = {
			tree = german_focus_hitler_himmler_coup
			keep_completed = yes
		}
		remove_ideas_with_trait = frnmin
		remove_ideas_with_trait = ecomin
		remove_ideas_with_trait = secmin
		remove_ideas_with_trait = intmin
		add_ideas = GER_SS_Disloyal_Military
		retire_character = GER_rudolf_hess
		retire_character = GER_hermann_goring
		retire_character = GER_martin_bormann
		set_country_flag = GER_Himmler_Coup
		add_ideas = {
			himmler2
			schulenberg
			himmler
			ernst_kaltenbrunner
			oswald_pohl
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = "GER_heinrich_himmler"
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = nazism
				expire = "1965.1.1.1"
				traits = {}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = fascism
				name = GER_SS_party
				long_name = GER_SS_party_long
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_internal.92.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		add_stability = -0.10
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = himmler
			}
			remove_ideas = himmler
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = schulenberg
			}
			remove_ideas = schulenberg
			add_ideas = ribbentrop
		}
		retire_character = GER_heinrich_himmler
		hidden_effect = {
			WGR = { retire_character = GER_heinrich_himmler }
		}
		set_country_flag = GER_pohl_incapable
		set_country_flag = GER_kaltenbrunner_incapable
		set_country_flag = GER_schulenberg_incapable
		set_country_flag = GER_SS_destroyed
		delete_unit_template_and_units = {
			division_template = "SS-Panzerdivision"
		}
		delete_unit_template_and_units = {
			division_template = "SS-Panzergrenadier-Division"
		}
		delete_unit_template_and_units = {
			division_template = "SS-Gebirgs-Division"
		}
		delete_unit_template_and_units = {
			division_template = "SS-Kavallerie-Division"
		}
	}
}

		add_ideas = GER_SPA_Econ_Assistance_GER
		SPN = {
			add_state_claim = 118
			add_state_claim = 751
			add_state_claim = 31 # replace w/roussilion
			add_state_claim = 461
			add_state_claim = 459
			country_event = {
				id = ger_external.22
			}
			add_ideas = GER_SPA_Econ_Assistance
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_external.21.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 99
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = SPN modifier = GER_didnt_join_faction }
		SPN = {
			country_event = {
				id = ger_external.23
			}
		}
	}
}

###July Putsch Path
#41: Hans Frank Incident - threatenes Austria
#42: Austria gets ^
#43: Hans Frank Incident - deported or let him speak
#44: Germany gets result of ^ - deported
#45: Frindolin Glass subordinates his unit (Vienna SS-Standarte 89) directly to Berlin causing tention with the SA
#46: Coup Begins Planning
#47: Hitler has to agree to it, triggers 48
#48: The coup event, can either turn Austria Fascist or Coup fails (but kills Chancellor), leading to the standoff - historical
#49: Germany is informed of Success and Annexes Austria
#50: Germany is informed of failure and gets choice to threaten (51) or let it go
#51: Austria gets threat, can call in Italy to defend maybe
#52: Germany gets Austrian submission
#53: Italy responds to ^
#54: Austria Gets italy's agreement
#55: Austria Gets Italy's refusal
#56: Germany is informed of the situation, can either back down or invade
#57: Austria and Italy gets back down message

country_event = { #Hans Frank Incident
	id = ger_external.41
	title = ger_external.41.t
	desc = ger_external.41.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Frank_Himmler_and_Daluege #Frank_Himmler_Daluege
	trigger = { #This will be replaced by an is_triggered_only = yes when Austrian content is more complete. OTL this incident was started because of the start of Austrofascism.
		tag = GER
		has_government = fascism
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		country_exists = AUS
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = AUS
			AUS = { is_subject = yes }
			AUS = { has_government = fascism }
		}
		# date > 1933.3.5
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			AUS = {
				set_country_flag = GER_Hans_Frank_Thretened_Austria
				country_event = {
					id = ger_external.42
					hours = 6
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ger_external.43 #Hans Frank tour
					days = 66
					random_days = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_external.41.a
		add_stability = -0.005
		add_political_power = -15
	}
}

country_event = { #Austria gets ^
	id = ger_external.42
	title = ger_external.42.t
	desc = ger_external.42.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_speech
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_external.42.a
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = GER
			modifier = AUS_hans_frank_threaten
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = fascism
			popularity = 0.02
		}
		add_stability = -0.01
	}
}

country_event = { #Hans Frank Tour
	id = ger_external.43
	title = ger_external.43.t
	desc = ger_external.43.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_speech
	trigger = {
		tag = AUS
		NOT = {
			has_government = fascism
			has_war_with = GER
			is_subject_of = GER
		}
		has_country_flag = GER_Hans_Frank_Thretened_Austria
		# date > 1933.5.10
	}
	fire_only_once = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			AUS = {
				country_event = {
					id = ger_external.42
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Deport Him!
		name = ger_external.43.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = fascism
			popularity = 0.01
		}
		add_stability = -0.01
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = GER
			modifier = AUS_hans_frank_agitated
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			GER_hans_frank = {
				set_nationality = GER
			}
			clr_country_flag = GER_Hans_Frank_Thretened_Austria
			GER = {
				country_event = {
					id = ger_external.44
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = { #Let Him Speak
		name = ger_external.43.b
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = GER_Hans_Frank_Thretened_Austria
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = fascism
			popularity = 0.03
		}
		add_stability = -0.03
	}
}

	id = ger_external.44
	title = ger_external.44.t
	desc = ger_external.44.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Papen_in_Munich #Papen_in_Munich
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_external.44.a
		add_political_power = -10
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = AUS
			modifier = AUS_hans_frank_deported
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #Coup Plans
	id = ger_external.46
	title = ger_external.46.t
	desc = ger_external.46.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_fascist_gathering
	trigger = {
		tag = GER
		has_government = fascism
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		country_exists = AUS
		NOT = {
			has_war_with = AUS
			AUS = { is_subject = yes }
			AUS = { has_government = fascism }
		}
		has_country_flag = GER_Frindolin_Submits_Unit
	}
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

country_event = { #Germany is informed of Success and Annexes Austria
	id = ger_external.49
	title = ger_external.49.t
	desc = ger_external.49.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Hitler_Enters_Austria #Hitler_enters_Austria
	is_triggered_only = yes
	#fire_only_once = yes
	option = { #Turns Austria Fascist
		name = ger_external.49.a
		hidden_effect = {
			AUS = {
				remove_country_leader_role = {
					character = "AUS_arthur_seyss_inquart"
					ideology = nazism
				}
				AUS_arthur_seyss_inquart = {
					set_nationality = GER
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_character = GER_hans_frank
					}
					GER_hans_frank = {
						set_nationality = GER
					}
				}
			}
		}
		annex_country = {
			target = AUS
			transfer_troops = yes
		}
		every_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = AUS
			}
			add_core_of = GER
		}
		add_stability = 0.05
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = fascism
			popularity = 0.05
		}
		set_country_flag = GER_Anchsluss_done
		set_global_flag = GER_annexed_austria
	}
}

country_event = { # Munich Conference - Germany - cze refuses
	id = ger_external.70
	title = ger_external.70.t
	desc = ger_external.70.d
	picture = GFX_Events_Germany_Czechs_on_Patrol #Czechs_on_Patrol
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	
	option = { # fight
		name = ger_external.70.a
		set_country_flag = GER_cze_does_not_surrender
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 80
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.8
				strength_ratio = { tag = CZE ratio < 0.8 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.4
				strength_ratio = { tag = ENG ratio < 0.8 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.4
				strength_ratio = { tag = FRA ratio < 0.8 }
			}
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			type = annex_everything
			target = CZE
		}
	}
	
	option = { # backdown
		name = ger_external.70.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				strength_ratio = { tag = CZE ratio > 0.8 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				strength_ratio = { tag = ENG ratio > 0.7 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				strength_ratio = { tag = FRA ratio > 0.7 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = hitler
			}
			add_stability = -0.10
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = fascism
				popularity = -0.10
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_stability = -0.10
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = nationalism
				popularity = -0.05
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = monarchism
				popularity = -0.05
			}
		}
	}
}

				is_in_faction = yes
				factor = 0.5
			}
		}
		GER = { country_event = { id = ger_external.82 days = 1 } }
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_war_support = 0.05
	}

		name = ger_external.81.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60
			modifier = {
				strength_ratio = { tag = GER ratio < 0.7 }
				factor = 0.7
			}
			modifier = {
				strength_ratio = { tag = GER ratio < 0.5 }
				factor = 0.7
			}
			modifier = {
				strength_ratio = { tag = GER ratio < 0.34 }
				factor = 0.7
			}
		}
		GER = { country_event = { id = ger_external.83 days = 1 } }
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_war_support = 0.10
	}
}

country_event = { # GER tries killing Horthy
	id = ger_external.84
	title = ger_external.84.t
	desc = ger_external.84.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Austria_Federal_Army_in_Vienna #Vienna_Federal_Army
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = { # submit to the arrow cross
		name = ger_external.84.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.2
				factor = 2
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.4
				factor = 3
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.6
				factor = 4
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.8
				factor = 5
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = fascism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_party_name = { ideology = fascism long_name = HUN_arrow_cross_party_long name = HUN_arrow_cross_party }
		drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
		set_cosmetic_tag = HUN_Arrow_Cross
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { is_subject = yes }
				NOT = { is_subject_of = GER }
			}
			GER = { country_event = { id = ger_external.85 days = 1 } }
		}
		set_country_flag = GER_arrow_cross_coup
		add_stability = -0.20
		if = {
			limit = { fascism > 0.14 }
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = fascism
				popularity = -0.10
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # resist
		name = ger_external.84.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 99
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.2
				factor = 0.8
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.4
				factor = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.6
				factor = 0.5
			}
			modifier = {
				surrender_progress > 0.8
				factor = 0.5
			}
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.15 }
		add_stability = -0.25
		add_manpower = -200
		43 = { add_manpower = -750 }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction_with = GER }
			GER = { remove_from_faction = HUN }
		}
		GER = { country_event = { id = ger_external.86 days = 1 } }
	}
}

country_event = { # demand switzerland - yes
	id = ger_external.88
	title = ger_external.88.t
	desc = ger_external.88.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_Hans_von_Seeckt
	
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = { # annex swi
		name = ger_external.88.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
		}
		annex_country = { target = SWI }
		add_state_core = 3
		add_state_core = 151
		816 = { set_state_name = "Romandien" }
		add_political_power = 20
		add_named_threat = { threat = 7 name = GER_annexed_swi }
	}
	option = { # partition swi
		name = ger_external.88.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				FRA = {
					is_neighbor_of = GER
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = GER
					}
				}
				ITA = {
					is_neighbor_of = GER
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = GER
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_named_threat = { threat = 7 name = GER_annexed_swi }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
		}
		annex_country = { target = SWI }
		add_state_core = 3
		add_state_core = 151
		if = {
			limit = {
				ITA = {
					is_neighbor_of = GER
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = GER
					}
				}
			}
			ITA = {
				country_event = {
					id = ger_external.90
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = {
					is_neighbor_of = GER
					NOT = {
						has_war_with = GER
					}
				}
			}
			FRA = {
				country_event = {
					id = ger_external.91
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # SVA - HUN - ROM refuses - GER Goes to War
	id = ger_external.95
	title = ger_external.95.t
	desc = ger_external.95.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Hungarians_Parade_Transylvania #Hungarians_Parade_Transylvania
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = { # join germany
		name = ger_external.95.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 99 }
		add_state_claim = 76
		if = {
			limit = {
				GER = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				NOT = { HUN = { is_in_faction = yes } }
			}
			GER = { add_to_faction = HUN }
			HUN = { set_country_flag = axis_country }
		}
		add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = GER enemy = ROM }
		add_named_threat = { threat = 5 name = HUN_sva }
	}
	option = { # not join germany
		name = ger_external.95.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
		add_political_power = -100
	}
}

country_event = { #GER - POL signs the NAP2 in exchange for DZG
	id = ger_external.118
	title = ger_external.118.t
	desc = ger_external.118.d
	picture = GFX_Events_Germany_Germans_Danzig #Germans_Danzig
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_external.118.a
		diplomatic_relation = { country = POL relation = non_aggression_pact }
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = DZG
				DZG = { is_subject_of = POL }
			}
			annex_country = { target = DZG }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { POL = { controls_state = 773 } }
			transfer_state = 773
		}
		add_state_core = 773
	}
}

country_event = { # GER-SOV alliance
	id = ger_external.136
	title = ger_external.136.t
	desc = ger_external.136.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_german_speech #Stalin_Ribbentrop
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = { # yes
		name = ger_external.136.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			#modifier = {
			#	GER = { has_country_flag = GER_strasser_in_power }
			#	factor = 1.2
			#}
		}
		dismantle_faction = yes
		add_named_threat = { threat = 4 name = GER_unholy_alliance }
		GER = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_in_faction_with = GER
					NOT = {
						has_government = communism
						has_government = authoritarian_socialism
						has_government = socialism
					}
				}
				country_event = { id = ger_external.139 }
			}
			add_named_threat = { threat = 4 name = GER_unholy_alliance }
			add_to_faction = SOV
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_government = communism
					has_government = authoritarian_socialism
					has_government = socialism
				}
				add_opinion_modifier = {
					target = SOV
					modifier = SOV_works_with_germans
				}
			}
			country_event = { id = ger_external.137 }
		}	
	}
	
	option = { # no
		name = ger_external.136.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		GER = {
			country_event = { id = ger_external.138 }
		}	
	}
}

	option = { # give 188 to germany
		name = ger_external.146.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = {
				FROM = { surrender_progress > 0.1 }
				factor = 0.1111
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			transfer_state = 188
			country_event = { id = ger_external.147 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					188 = {
						has_state_flag = GER_MR
					}
				}
				188 = { clr_state_flag = GER_MR }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # refuse
		name = ger_external.146.b
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10
			modifier = {
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = ger_external.148 }
		}
	}
}

				}

			}
		}
		FROM = {

			}
		}

			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				tempRand = 100
			}
			round_variable = tempRand
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						tempRand < 36.01
					}
				}
				POL = {
					country_event = {
						id = ger_external.163 #Poland found out
						hours = 8
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_external.160.b
		FROM = {
			country_event = {
				id = ger_external.162
				days = 1
			}
		}
	}

country_event = { # Austro-German Customs Union DNVP
	id = ger_external.170
	title = ger_external.170.t
	desc = ger_external.170.d
	picture = GFX_Event_Germany_Papen_in_Munich
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = { #Austria agrees
		name = ger_external.170.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 90
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_government = communism
					has_government = authoritarian_socialism
				}
				factor = 0
			}
			modifier = {
				has_government = fascism
				factor = 0.2
			}
			modifier = {
				OR = {
					has_government = socialism
					has_government = social_democracy
					has_government = neutrality
					has_government = monarchism
					has_government = nationalism
				}
				factor = 0.8
			}
			modifier = {
				is_in_faction = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_guaranteed = AUS
			}
			diplomatic_relation = { country = AUS relation = guarantee active = no }
			set_country_flag = AUS_left_sphere_flag
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_military_access_to = AUS
			}
			diplomatic_relation = { country = AUS relation = military_access active = no }
			AUS = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = military_access active = no } }
			set_country_flag = AUS_left_sphere_flag
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = AUS_left_sphere_flag
			}
			clr_country_flag = AUS_left_sphere_flag
			add_opinion_modifier = { target = AUS modifier = AUS_left_sphere }
			country_event = { id = ger_external.173 }
		}
		FROM = {

country_event = { #Hitler Speech in the International Automobile and Motorcycle Exhibition in Berlin; 11 Feb 1933
	id = ger_projects.1
	title = ger_projects.1.t
	desc = ger_projects.1.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_1933_berlin_motor_show

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = ger_projects.2
				days = 224
				hours = 5
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_projects.1.a
		unlock_decision_category_tooltip = GER_reichsautobahn_Projects
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = Nazi_Autobahn_category_start }
	}
}

country_event = { #September 23 1933 in Frankfurt; Unemployed march and Hitler innaugurates auobahn work with the first shoveling of dirt in the Frank-Darmst section.
	id = ger_projects.2
	title = ger_projects.2.t
	desc = ger_projects.2.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_first_shoveling_of_dirt

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = {
				id = ger_projects.3
				days = 179
			}
			country_event = {
				id = ger_projects.4
				days = 2
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_projects.2.a
		set_variable = { autobahnen_value = 0 }
		set_variable = { num_civ_autobahn_use = 2 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = we_can_begin_autobahn_building_tt
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = GER_Reichsautobahnen_construction_cost
		}
		activate_decision = GER_Frankfurt_Darmstadt_bahn
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = fascism
			popularity = 0.05
		}
		add_political_power = 20
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = GER_start_autobahn_building }
	}
}

country_event = { #Endsieg Party Faction Part 0 - setup
	id = ger_endsieg.19
	title = ger_endsieg.19.t
	desc = ger_endsieg.19.d
	picture = GFX_Events_Germany_Goebbels_Speech #Goebbels_Speech
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = ger_endsieg.19.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		set_variable = { GER_endsieg_congress = 1 } # by the end, >0 signifies a radical victory, and <0 signifies a conservative victory. Yes, the conservatives are favored.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_Hitler_Victory_Politics_Kirchenkampf_Reich_Church
			}
			subtract_from_variable = {
				var = GER_endsieg_congress
				value = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = GER_Hitler_Victory_Politics_Kirchenkampf_Rosenberg
			}
			add_to_variable = {
				var = GER_endsieg_congress
				value = 1
			}
		}
		country_event = {
			id = ger_endsieg.20
			days = 7
		}
	}
}

		has_country_flag = GER_Military_Coup

			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler2
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler3
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler4
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler5
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 100
		modifier = {
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler2
			factor = 1.05
		}
		modifier = {
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler3
			factor = 1.1
		}
		modifier = {
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler4
			factor = 1.15
		}
		modifier = {
			has_idea = Legacy_of_Hitler5
			factor = 1.2
		}
		modifier = {
			fascism > 0.30
			factor = 0.75
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_antinazi.4.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		random_owned_state = {
			set_temp_variable = {
				tempRand = random
			}
			multiply_temp_variable = {
				tempRand = 100
			}
			round_variable = tempRand
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						tempRand < 50.01
					}
				}
				damage_building = {
					type = infrastructure
					damage = 0.1
				}
			}
			else = {
				damage_building = {
					type = industrial_complex
					damage = 0.1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		tag = GER

		OR = {
			has_idea = GER_MEFO_Idea_20
			has_idea = GER_MEFO_Failed
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 200
		modifier = {
			date < 1940.1.1
			factor = 1.25
		}
		modifier = {
			date < 1942.1.1
			factor = 1.25
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_himmler.11.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = GER_MEFO_Idea_20
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = GER_MEFO_Idea_20
				add_idea = GER_MEFO_Endsieg_Bankrupcy
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = GER_MEFO_Failed
			}
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = GER_MEFO_Failed
				add_idea = GER_MEFO_Endsieg_Bankrupcy
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_ideas = GER_MEFO_Failed
		}
	}
}

# Germany Quits League of Nations and World Disarmament Conference - 14 of October 1933
news_event = {
	id = ger_news.20
	title = ger_news.20.t
	desc = ger_news.20.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_hitler_speech_crowd
	is_triggered_only = yes
	major = yes

	trigger = {
		GER = {
			has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		}
	}

	show_major = {
		GER = {
			has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { tag = GER }
				complete_national_focus = GER_remove_league
				news_event = { #World Disarmament Conference Adjourns
					id = ger_news.21
					days = 382
					random_days = 15
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { 
			tag = GER
		}
		name = ger_news.20.a
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { 
			NOT = { tag = GER }
		}
		name = ger_news.20.b
	}
}

# Hitler Dies - Historical

	id = ger_news.100
	title = ger_news.100.t
	desc = ger_news.100.desc
	picture = GFX_news_event_german_reichstag_ruins_02
	is_triggered_only = yes
	major = yes

	option = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = GER
				is_in_faction_with = GER
				tag = GER
				is_subject_of = GER
			}
		}
		name = ger_news.100.a
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_news.100.b
		trigger = {
			has_government = communism
			OR = {
				has_war_with = GER
				tag = SOV #In case SOV is not at war with GER, it will still say this
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_news.100.c
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_government = communism}
			has_war_with = GER
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_news.100.d
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				is_in_faction_with = GER
				is_subject_of = GER
				tag = GER
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_triggered_only = yes

		has_government = fascism
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = GRI
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_flavor.20.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ger_restore_imperial_tt
		add_political_power = 5
	}
}

#This dual flag arrangement ended on 15 September 1935, one year after the death of Reich President Paul von Hindenburg, and the Nazi flag became the only national flag of Germany. 
#One reason for the change may have been the "Bremen incident" of 26 July 1935, in which a group of demonstrators in New York City boarded the ocean liner SS Bremen, tore the Nazi Party flag from the jackstaff, and tossed it into the Hudson River. 
#When the German ambassador protested, US officials responded that the German national flag had not been harmed, only a political party symbol.
#The new flag law was announced at the annual party rally in Nuremberg in 1935, where Hermann Göring claimed the old black-white-red flag, while honoured, was the symbol of a bygone era and under threat of being used by "reactionaries".
country_event = { #Nazi Flag time - 15 of September 1935
	id = ger_flavor.21
	title = ger_flavor.21.t
	desc = ger_flavor.21.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_fascist_gathering
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
			set_cosmetic_tag = GER_Reich
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = ger_flavor.21.a
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 100
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = ger_nazi_flag_tt
		add_political_power = 5
	}
}

# Launch of the Hindenburg (Historical) 3/11/1935
country_event = {
	id = ger_flavor.30
	title = ger_flavor.30.t
	desc = ger_flavor.30.desc
	picture = x

	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		#US doesn't sell Helium to Germany
		NOT = { has_global_flag = GCW_ongoing_flag }
		GER = { 
			has_war = no
			has_country_flag = GER_hitler_in_power
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = {
				80 = {
					country_event = {
						id = ger_flavor.1
						days = 1036
					}
				}
				20 = {
					country_event = {
						id = ger_flavor.2
						days = 1036
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = ger_flavor.30.a
	}
}
